===== .deps/p2a-fv-l01/after_old_cache.py =====
SHA256 93dc0a95bd2917a162880b004eb250cbe3d38128e47340e610d961821acaf225
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,shlex
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-fv-l01';e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(root/'.deps/cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/'before-target'))
for k in ('CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO','RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER'):e.pop(k,None)
c=['cargo','clippy','--offline','--locked','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml','--all-targets','--','-D','warnings']
p=subprocess.run(c,env=e,capture_output=True,text=True);(w/'after-old-cache.log').write_text('$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n'+p.stdout+p.stderr+'\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n');print('existing before-correction failed cache, after correction:',p.returncode)
(w/'after-old-cache-result.json').write_text(json.dumps(dict(exit=p.returncode,cache=json.loads((w/'before-target/.rustc_info.json').read_text())),indent=2));assert p.returncode==0

===== .deps/p2a-fv-l01/before.py =====
SHA256 a38f7079f52fb419e7150d1a68726cfc0a39c6966110818b53edb7e8bdd84aa7
from pathlib import Path
import os,json,subprocess,hashlib,shlex
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-fv-l01'
paths=subprocess.check_output(['git','ls-files','-z'],text=True).split('\0')
paths+= [str(p.relative_to(root)) for p in (root/'docs').glob('PHASE_2A_ASTRA*')]
(w/'start-hashes.json').write_text(json.dumps({p:hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in paths if p and (root/p).is_file()},indent=2))
target=w/'before-target';target.mkdir()
rsp=w/'duplicate.rsp';rsp.write_text('--cfg=getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"\n')
env=os.environ.copy();env.update(CARGO_HOME=str(root/'.deps/cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(target))
for k in ('CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO','RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER'):env.pop(k,None)
base=['cargo','clippy','--offline','--locked','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml'];rs=[]
for name,extra in [('before-invalid',['--','@'+str(rsp)]),('before-valid',['--all-targets','--','-D','warnings'])]:
 c=base+extra;p=subprocess.run(c,env=env,capture_output=True,text=True)
 (w/(name+'.log')).write_text('$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n'+p.stdout+p.stderr+'\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 cache=target/'.rustc_info.json';rs.append(dict(name=name,command=c,exit=p.returncode,cache=json.loads(cache.read_text()) if cache.exists() else None));print(name,p.returncode)
(w/'before-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rs,indent=2));assert all(x['exit']!=0 for x in rs)

import os,subprocess,json,shlex,time
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;root=w.parent.parent
e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'verified-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/'final-normal-target'))
for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):e.pop(k,None)
assert not Path(e['CARGO_TARGET_DIR']).exists()
a=['--offline','--locked','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml']
cmds=[['cargo','fmt','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml','--check'],['cargo','clippy',*a,'--all-targets','--','-D','warnings'],['cargo','test',*a],['cargo','test',*a,'--release']]
rs=[]
for i,c in enumerate(cmds):
 t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'final-normal-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n');f.flush();p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=e,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT);f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 rs.append(dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),environment={k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')}));(w/'final-normal-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(rs,indent=2));print(rs[-1],flush=True)
 assert p.returncode==0


===== .deps/p2a-fv-l01/final_policy.py =====
SHA256 4a53b7a93a94712e784e17e68a036f3a1cf37c3ae420815679c3d6fcb3831bf3
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures,hashlib
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;root=w.parent.parent
frozen={str(p):hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() for p in [root/'scripts/guard_rustc.py',root/'scripts/check_rng_policy.py']}
(w/'final-policy-source-hashes.json').write_text(json.dumps(frozen,indent=2))
commands={4:['python3','scripts/check_rng_policy.py'],5:['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],10:['make','check']};results={}
def run(i):
 e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(w/'verified-cargo-home'),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/f'final-policy-target-{i}'))
 assert not Path(e['CARGO_TARGET_DIR']).exists()
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):e.pop(k,None)
 c=commands[i];t=time.monotonic()
 with (w/f'final-policy-{i}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n');f.flush();p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=e,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT);f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),environment={k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=3) as pool:
 for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed([pool.submit(run,i) for i in commands]):
  i,r=future.result();results[i]=r;(w/'final-policy-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
assert all(hashlib.sha256(Path(p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()==h for p,h in frozen.items())
assert len(results)==3 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())

===== .deps/p2a-fv-l01/preservation.py =====
SHA256 7eabb62b383400d51b7d9ba355a2e876e09d2de8d298ca0e3d515b7474ee0cb2
from pathlib import Path
import json,hashlib,subprocess,re
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-fv-l01';before=json.loads((w/'start-hashes.json').read_text())
changed=[p for p,h in before.items() if not (root/p).is_file() or hashlib.sha256((root/p).read_bytes()).hexdigest()!=h]
expected={'scripts/guard_rustc.py','scripts/check_rng_policy.py','docs/DECISIONS.md','docs/ENVIRONMENT.md','docs/KNOWN_LIMITATIONS.md','docs/TEST_MATRIX.md','docs/PHASE_2A_REVIEW.md'}
assert set(changed)==expected,changed
matrix=(root/'docs/TEST_MATRIX.md').read_text();rows=[l for l in matrix.splitlines() if l.startswith('| PKI-')]
head=subprocess.check_output(['git','show','HEAD:docs/TEST_MATRIX.md'],text=True)
assert rows==[l for l in head.splitlines() if l.startswith('| PKI-')] and len(rows)==19 and all('| PLANNED |' in l for l in rows)
ffi=(root/'trusted-client/src/pqc/ffi.rs').read_text();assert len(re.findall(r'unsafe extern',ffi))==1 and len(re.findall(r'unsafe \{',ffi))==10
headers=[]
for p in (w/'final-normal-target').glob('**/oqsconfig.h'):
 t=p.read_text();lines=['/* #undef OQS_USE_SHA3_AVX512VL */','/* #undef OQS_USE_OPENSSL */','#define OQS_USE_PTHREADS 1','#define OQS_ENABLE_KEM_ml_kem_768 1','#define OQS_ENABLE_SIG_ml_dsa_65 1']
 assert all(s in t for s in lines);headers.append(dict(path=str(p.relative_to(root)),sha256=hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),lines=lines))
assert len(headers)>=4
pin=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','.deps/liboqs-0.16.0','rev-parse','HEAD'],text=True).strip();assert pin=='5a1a854b0dc9f2141bdc771c555ee60c37950183'
status=subprocess.check_output(['git','-C','.deps/liboqs-0.16.0','status','--porcelain','--untracked-files=all','--ignored=matching'],text=True);assert status==''
report=dict(allowed_changed_start_files=changed,all_other_start_hashes_unchanged=True,pki_planned=19,unsafe_extern=1,unsafe_calls=10,backend_commit=pin,backend_source_status=status,headers=headers)
(w/'preservation.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2));print(json.dumps({k:v for k,v in report.items() if k!='headers'},indent=2));print('verified configuration headers',len(headers))

SHA256 10403007ee630dec4f53c8f854ae649ed3effbd62aa19386dbd84c5a1036b499
from pathlib import Path
import os,subprocess,json,time,shlex,concurrent.futures
w=Path(__file__).resolve().parent;root=w.parent.parent
home=w/'verified-cargo-home';target=w/'verified-target'
home.mkdir();assert not list(home.iterdir()) and not target.exists()
a=['--offline','--locked','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml']
commands=[['cargo','fmt','--manifest-path','trusted-client/Cargo.toml','--check'],['cargo','clippy',*a,'--all-targets','--','-D','warnings'],['cargo','test',*a],['cargo','test',*a,'--release'],['python3','scripts/check_rng_policy.py'],['python3','scripts/validate_phase2a.py'],['python3','scripts/validate_phase1.py'],['python3','scripts/check_pqc_native.py'],['python3','scripts/check_pqc_source_pin.py'],['make','build'],['make','check'],['git','diff','--check']]
results={}
def run(i):
 e=os.environ.copy();e.update(CARGO_HOME=str(home),CARGO_TARGET_DIR=str(w/f'parallel-target-{i}' if i in (4,5,10) else target))
 for k in ('RUSTFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTFLAGS','RUSTDOCFLAGS','CARGO_ENCODED_RUSTDOCFLAGS','RUSTC_WRAPPER','RUSTC_WORKSPACE_WRAPPER','CLIPPY_ARGS','CARGO_CACHE_RUSTC_INFO'):e.pop(k,None)
 t=time.monotonic();c=commands[i]
 with (w/f'full-{i:02}.log').open('w') as f:
  f.write('ENV '+json.dumps({k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})+'\n$ '+shlex.join(c)+'\n');f.flush()
  p=subprocess.run(c,cwd=root,env=e,stdout=f,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
  f.write('\nexit='+str(p.returncode)+'\n')
 return i,dict(command=c,exit=p.returncode,seconds=round(time.monotonic()-t,2),environment={k:e[k] for k in ('CARGO_HOME','CARGO_TARGET_DIR')})
def save(i,r):
 results[i]=r;(w/'full-results.json').write_text(json.dumps(results,indent=2));print(i,r,flush=True)
for i in range(4):
 j,r=run(i);save(j,r)
 if r['exit']!=0:raise SystemExit(1)
# Native/source checks share the verified target sequentially; policy suites use
# independent fresh targets and do not mutate production/source files.
def short_remaining():
 for i in (6,7,8,9,11):yield run(i)
with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4) as pool:
 futures=[pool.submit(run,i) for i in (4,5,10)]
 for i,r in short_remaining():save(i,r)
 for future in concurrent.futures.as_completed(futures):save(*future.result())
assert len(results)==12 and all(r['exit']==0 for r in results.values())

from pathlib import Path
import sys,os,subprocess,json
root=Path.cwd();w=root/'.deps/p2a-fv-l01';sys.path.insert(0,str(root/'scripts'))
from check_rng_policy import clippy_cache_sequences,wrapper_forwarding
work=w/'targeted-fixtures';work.mkdir()
files={}
for name,body in [('duplicate','--cfg=getrandom_backend="linux_getrandom"'),('custom','--cfg=getrandom_backend="custom"')]:
 p=work/(name+'.rsp');p.write_text(body+'\n');files[name]=p
e=os.environ.copy();e['CARGO_HOME']=str(root/'.deps/cargo-home')
wrapper_forwarding(work,e);clippy_cache_sequences(work,e,files)
